`compute_smooth_direction(h, cvec, temp, eps_bound=1e-7)`:
```
def compute_smooth_direction(h,cvec,temp):
    n = len(h); m=cvec.max()
    if temp <= 1e-12: weights = (cvec >= m - 1e-8).astype(float)
    else: weights=np.exp((cvec-m)/temp)
    tot=weights.sum()
    if tot <=0: return np.zeros(n)
    weights /= tot
    g=np.zeros(n)
    # loop s from -(n-1) to n-1
    for idx in range(len(cvec)):
        w=float(weights[idx])
        if w == 0.0: continue
        s = idx - (n-1)
        if s >= 0:
            r=s; nv=n-r
            if nv<=0: continue
            g[:nv] += w*(1.0 - h[r:])
            g[r:] -= w*h[:nv]
        else:
            r=-s; nv=n-r
            g[r:] += w*(1.0 - h[:nv])
            g[:nv] -= w*h[r:]
    g -= g.mean()
    free = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
    d = -g
    d[~free]=0
    nf = free.sum()
    if nf==0: return np.zeros(n)
    d[free] -= d[free].mean()
    maxp = max(d.max(), -d.min())
    if maxp < 1e-14: return np.zeros(n)
    d /= maxp
    return d
```
But for temp small, weights underflow. Use temp values. If temp too small, only exact max maybe one lag; direction may reduce that lag but increase others; line search will handle. We can use active set: top K equal weights perhaps better. For each iteration, we can choose direction based on top K (e.g., 20) with weights equally, K maybe min(20, len). Computing gradient with sparse top K faster. We can sweep K.

```
def active_direction(h,cvec,K):
   n=len; idx=np.argsort(cvec)[-K:]  # ascending, take last K
   w=np.zeros(len); w[idx]=1.0/K
   g=zeros; loop over idx only.
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100):
  t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # initialize
  try: h=initial_h_values... n=len
  except: h=np.full(500,0.5)
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  cvec=compute_cvec(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_cvec=cvec.copy(); best_c=compute_c5
  # local improve on current
  h,cvec,stopped = local_search(h,cvec,rng,deadline, work)
  update best if improved
  # maybe multi-n
  for n2 in [800,900,700? 600,1000]:
    if time > deadline - 250: break
    if n2 == n: continue
    h2=interp_project
    c2vec=compute_cvec
    h2,c2vec = local_search(limited deadline reserve)
    if c2 < best_c: adopt and set n=n2 (need maybe rescoping)
  # final polish on best
  h=interp back to original? If best at n2, polish.
  project and compute final.
  return h,best_c,n
```

Now, code structure top-level. Let's write mentally and count lines. Need <=300. We can be concise.

Functions:
1. `compute_cvec(h,n)`:
```
def compute_cvec(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
```
2. `compute_c5_from_cvec(cvec,n): return float(cvec.max()*(2.0/n))`
3. `project_feasible(h,n)`: use clips and loop. Concise:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*room,0,1)
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-15: break
          h=np.clip(h+diff/tot*h,0,1)
    return h
```
4. `line_max_at(t,A,B,C)`: `return float(np.max(C + B*t + A*t*t))`
5. `golden_line(lo,hi,A,B,C,span_tol=1e-7)`: top-level. Uses phi. If lo>hi return (lo+hi)/2. Iterate 60 or hi-lo > tol*max(1,abs(hi-lo)). But F nonunimodal; still local. Use `f1>f2` logic. Need line_max_at call creates array; okay.
6. `try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline)`: top-level. Need return h,cvec,improved.
Implementation details:
```
def try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline):
    n=len(h)
    if time.time()>deadline: return h,cvec,False
    pos=d>1e-14; neg=d<-1e-14
    if not np.any(pos) or not np.any(neg): return ...
    lo = max(np.max(-h[pos]/d[pos]), np.max((h[neg]-1.0)/d[neg]))
    hi = min(np.min((1.0-h[pos])/d[pos]), np.min(h[neg]/(-d[neg])))
    span=hi-lo
    if not (lo < hi) or span < 1e-9: return
    # ensure 0 feasible with margin
    lo = max(lo, -1e-12)? Actually 0 inside; due numerical if lo=1e-14 maybe. We can clamp lo = min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0)
    lo = min(lo, 0.0); hi = max(hi, 0.0); span=hi-lo
    if span < 1e-10: return
    t1=lo+0.25*span; t2=lo+0.75*span
    if abs(t1)<1e-12 or abs(t2)<1e-12: ... maybe use 0.2,0.8
    h1=h+t1*d; h2=h+t2*d
    # if out of bounds due to numerical, clamp t? Maybe adjust: if h1.min()<0 or h1.max()>1: shrink t? Simpler np.clip.
    if h1.min() < -1e-9 or h1.max() > 1+1e-9: return
    if h2.min() < -1e-9 or h2.max() > 1+1e-9: return
    h1=np.clip(h1,0,1); h2=np.clip(h2,0,1)
    c1=np.correlate(h1, 1.0-h1, mode='full'); c2=...
    C=cvec
    Delta1=c1-C; Delta2=c2-C
    A=(Delta1/t1 - Delta2/t2)/(t1-t2)
    B=(Delta1 - A*t1*t1)/t1
    # If A/B contains nan/inf? check `if not np.all(np.isfinite(A+B+C))`
    # Sampling
    n_samp = 80 if n <= 900 else 100
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, n_samp)
    if lo < 0.0 < hi:
       ts = np.concatenate((ts, np.array([[0.0]]))).ravel()?? Simpler: if not close any, create unique? `ts = np.unique(np.concatenate((ts, np.zeros(1))))`
    # But concatenate with zeros(1) okay.
    # To avoid large allocation, maybe evaluate in chunks? size 101x1399 =141k, fine.
    F = np.empty(len(ts))
    for i,tv in enumerate(ts): F[i]=line_max_at(tv,A,B,C)  # Python loop 100*max vector each 1400, maybe okay but loop 100 per line. If 10k lines ->1M loops, maybe heavy. Use vectorized matrix faster in C? It still does 100*1400 in BLAS? Actually expression C + B*t + A*t^2 in loop is Python 100 vector ops; maybe 100*0.1ms=10ms/line, too much 10s for 1000. Matrix op `C[None,:] + B[None,:]*ts[:,None] + A[None,:]*ts[:,None]*ts[:,None]` does one big vector op in C maybe faster. Use matrix.
    mat = C[None,:] + B[None,:]*ts[:,None] + A[None,:]*(ts[:,None]**2)
    rowmax=mat.max(axis=1); i0=int(rowmax.argmin())
    # Initialize golden bracket:
    if i0 == 0: lo2=ts[0]; hi2=ts[1]
    elif i0 == len(ts)-1: lo2=ts[-2]; hi2=ts[-1]
    else: lo2=ts[i0-1]; hi2=ts[i0+1]
    # Golden
    t_best = gold lo2 hi2 (maybe if width>1e-9)
    # Also compare to sample points and 0

Active direction maybe dense; line interval small. To increase step size, we can sparsify dense direction: take top M positive and negative components, zero others, normalize. This yields a block exchange aligned with active gradient. This might be better. Implement `sparse_direction(d, m=8)`:

Direction sum may not zero; project by scaling positive and negative groups separately to equal sum? Simpler: after selecting, enforce sum zero by subtracting mean over selected? But that reintroduces small. Or choose counts: pos weights and neg weights scale to have sums equal: Let posSel, negSel. Compute sp=d_posSum, sn=-d_negSum. We want d_pos values proportional to d_pos but scale factor α, neg proportional to d_neg with β such that α*sp = β*sn (positive total mass). Choose α=1/sp, β=1/sn? Then pos sum=1, neg sum=-1. Values may exceed 1 scale; normalize max. Direction sum zero exactly. This moves mass from neg to pos along gradient. Good. Implement:
```
def sparsify_direction(d, k=6):
  pos_idx=np.where(d>0)[0]; neg_idx=np.where(d<0)[0]
  if len pos<1 or neg<1: return zeros
  if len(pos_idx)>k: pos_idx=pos_idx[np.argsort(d[pos_idx])[-k:]]
  if len(neg_idx)>k: neg_idx=neg_idx[np.argsort(d[neg_idx])[:k]]  # most negative
  out=zeros; out[pos_idx]=d[pos_idx]; out[neg_idx]=d[neg_idx]
  ps=out[pos_idx].sum(); ns=-out[neg_idx].sum()
  if ps<=0 or ns<=0: return
  out[pos_idx] *= 1.0/ps; out[neg_idx] *= -1.0/ns
  # Now sum zero. Normalize max_abs to 1.
  m=max(max(out[pos_idx]), -min(out[neg_idx])); if m>0 out/=m
  return out
```
Then line interval may be larger because selected cells have substantial gradient. Use active sparse direction instead of full active sometimes. This is likely powerful.

```
d = np.full(n, -1.0/(n-1) if up else 1.0/(n-1))
d[p] = 1.0 if up else -1.0
# sum zero: up: (n-1)*(-1/(n-1)) +1=0; down similar.
return d
```

`golden_line` implementation:
```
def golden_line(lo,hi,A,B,C):
    if hi <= lo: return 0.5*(lo+hi)
    phi=1.618033988749895
    a1=hi-(hi-lo)/phi
    a2=lo+(hi-lo)/phi
    f1=line_max_at(a1,A,B,C); f2=line_max_at(a2,A,B,C)
    for _ in range(50):
        if hi-lo < 1e-8*max(1.0,abs(lo),abs(hi)): break
        if f1 < f2:
            hi=a2; a2=a1; f2=f1; a1=hi-(hi-lo)/phi; f1=line_max_at(a1,A,B,C)
        else:
            lo=a1; a1=a2; f1=f2; a2=lo+(hi-lo)/phi; f2=line_max_at(a2,A,B,C)
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```
Because F may not be unimodal, but bracket from sample around local min should be okay. If there is another local min in bracket lower, golden may fail. We can skip golden entirely: coarse 101 samples + local fine 41 samples around best. This is robust and simpler, no unimodal assumption. Number evaluations 142 vector max per line. Now, local_search direction cycle. Let's write concrete:
```
def local_search(h,cvec,rng,deadline, max_noimp=2000):
    n=len(h)
    noimp=0; step=0; acc=0
    Kvals=(1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        step += 1
        if step % 6 == 0:
            p = (step * 2654435761) % max(1,n-1)
            d = adjacent_d(n,p)
        elif step % 6 == 1:
            if step % 2 == 0: d = random_block_d(rng,n,2 + step%6?)
            else: d = random_pair_d(rng,n)
        elif step % 6 == 3:
            # rebalance
            p = rng.randint(n)
            up = h[p] > 0.5 or rng.rand()<0.5
            d = rebalance_d(p,n,up)
        else:
            K = Kvals[(step//6) % len(Kvals)]
            base = active_direction(h,cvec,K)
            if step % 2 == 0:
                d = sparsify_direction(base, 2 + (step//6)%8)
            else:
                d = base
        if norm(d) small:
            d = random_pair_d(rng,n)
        h,cvec,imp = try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h))? break
        if imp:
            noimp=0; acc+=1
            # every 100 accepted, verify/project
            if acc % 100 == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h,n)
                cvec = compute_cvec(h,n)
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp > max_noimp:
                break
    return h,cvec
```

`sparsify_direction(base,k)` needs if base zero. k between 2 and 16. Implement:
```
def sparsify_direction(d,k):
    pos = d > 1e-14; neg = d < -1e-14
    if not pos.any() or not neg.any(): return d
    idxp = np.where(pos)[0]
    if len(idxp) > k:
        idxp = idxp[np.argsort(d[idxp], kind='stable')[-k:]]
    idxn = np.where(neg)[0]
    if len(idxn) > k:
        idxn = idxn[np.argsort(d[idxn], kind='stable')[:k]]
    out = np.zeros(len(d))
    out[idxp] = d[idxp]
    out[idxn] = d[idxn]
    ps = out[idxp].sum(); ns = -out[idxn].sum()
    if ps <= 1e-14 or ns <= 1e-14: return zero
    out[idxp] *= 1.0/ps; out[idxn] *= -1.0/ns
    # normalize max
    m = max(np.max(out[idxp]), -np.min(out[idxn]))
    if m <= 1e-14: return zeros
    out /= m
    return out
```
This creates `out` and slices. Good.

`adjacent_d(n,p)`: p from 0 to n-2. If p>=n-1, p=0. For speed, allocate zeros. Could compare to random. Good.

Now, compute `norm(d)`: `dmx = max(np.max(np.abs(d)),0)`. If <=1e-14 random. Since `d` allocated, `np.abs` overhead. Use `max(abs(float(d.max())), abs(float(d.min())))`.

Now, multi-n implementation. We need a helper `resample_h(old,n_new)`:
```
def resample_h(old,n_new):
   n_old=len(old)
   if n_old == n_new: return old.copy()
   x_old=(np.arange(n_old)+0.5)*2.0/n_old
   x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*2.0/n_new
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,old)
```
Then project, compute cvec.

In run:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except:
       n=700; h=np.full(n,0.5)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cvec=compute_cvec(h,n); best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cvec.max()*2.0/n
    # initial local
    dl = deadline - min(450, max(180, int(0.35*budget_s)))
    h,cvec=local_search(h,cvec,rng,dl,max_noimp=5000)
    c=float(cvec.max()*2.0/len(h))
    if c < best_c: best_h, best_c = h.copy(), c
    # multi-n list depending on time
    cands = [800, 900, 700, 600, 1000]
    # remove len(best_h), maybe include if not
    for nt in cands:
       if time.time() > deadline - 120: break
       if nt == len(best_h): continue
       if nt > 1400: continue
       slice_dl = time.time() + min(120, max(30, 0.10*budget_s))
       if slice_dl > deadline - 60: slice_dl = deadline - 60
       hn = resample_h(best_h, nt)
       hn = project_feasible(hn, nt)
       cn = compute_cvec(hn, nt)
       hn, cn = local_search(hn, cn, rng, slice_dl, max_noimp=1000)
       cnf = float(cn.max()*(2.0/nt))
       if cnf < best_c:
           h=...? best_h=hn.copy(); best_c=cnf; best_cvec=cn.copy()
       h=best_h.copy()
    # noise restarts
    for sigma in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05]:
       if time.time() > deadline - 80: break
       slice = time.time()+min(80, max(20,0.08*budget_s)) ...
       hn = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,len(best_h)), len(best_h))
       cn=compute_cvec(hn,...)
       hn,cn=local_search(hn,cn,rng,slice,max_noimp=500)
       cnf=...
       if cnf < best_c: update
    # final polish on best_h with remaining
    if time.time() < deadline - 10:
       h=best_h.copy(); cvec=compute_cvec(h,len(h))
       h,cvec=local_search(h,cvec,rng,deadline-5,max_noimp=2000)
       c=...
       if c<best_c: update
    # final recompute
    h_final=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h))
    cvec_final=compute_cvec(h_final,len(h_final))
    best_c=float(cvec_final.max()*(2.0/len(h_final)))
    return h_final,best_c,len(h_final)
```

def row_max_values(ts,A,B,C):
    t = ts[:,None]
    return np.max(C[None,:] + B[None,:]*t + A[None,:]*t*t, axis=1)

`active_direction`: Need `n1 = n - 1`.
```
def active_direction(h,cvec,K):
   n=len(h); m=cvec.size
   if K <=0: return zeros
   if K >= m:
       idx=np.arange(m); w=1.0/m
   elif K <= 8:
       idx=np.argsort(cvec)[-K:]
       w=1.0/K
   else:
       idx=np.argpartition(cvec, m-K)[-K:]
       w=1.0/K
   g=np.zeros(n)
   n1=n-1
   for idxi in idx:
       ii=int(idxi); wi=w
       if wi <=0: continue
       s=ii-n1
       if s>=0:
          r=s; nv=n-r
          if nv <=0: continue
          g[:nv] += wi*(1.0 - h[r:])
          g[r:] -= wi*h[:nv]
       else:
          r=-s; nv=n-r
          g[r:] += wi*(1.0 - h[:nv])
          g[:nv] -= wi*h[r:]
   g -= g.mean()
   free = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
   d = -g
   d[~free] = 0.0
   nf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
   if nf == 0: return d
   d[free] -= d[free].mean()
   dmax = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
   if dmax < 1e-14: return d
   return d/dmax
```
Note: For K via argpartition, idx may not sorted; weights equal. For `K=1`, argsort okay. If `M-K` equals M? if K=0, handled. If K=m, all. Good.
Potential performance: `d[free] -= d[free].mean()` modifies copy? `d[free]` is view? It returns copy for boolean? Actually boolean indexing returns copy, in-place subtract on copy maybe not modify d? In NumPy, `d[free] -= value` works (after copy, it writes back). Yes boolean indexing assignment works. But `d[free].mean()` okay. Could compute `sf = d[free].sum(); d[free] -= sf/nf`. Use that.

def project_direction(g,h):

   g -= g.mean()

`random_pair_d`:
```
def random_pair_d(rng,n):
   p = int(rng.randint(n)); q = int(rng.randint(n))
   if p == q: q = (p+1) % n
   d=np.zeros(n); d[p]=1.0; d[q]=-1.0; return d
```
No scaling needed.

`adjacent_d`:
```
def adjacent_d(n,p):
   if p >= n-1: p=0
   d=np.zeros(n); d[p]=1; d[p+1]=-1; return d
```

`block_direction`:
```
def block_direction(rng,n,b):
   b = int(b); if b<2: b=2; if b > n//2: b=max(2,n//2)
   lim = n-b
   p = int(rng.randint(lim+1)); q = int(rng.randint(lim+1))
   if q == p:
       q = (q+b) % (lim+1)
       if q == p and lim: q=(q+1)%(lim+1)
   d=np.zeros(n); d[p:p+b]=1.0; d[q:q+b]=-1.0
   return d
```
If p and q blocks partially overlap, sum still zero. If q=p exactly, blocks cancel; avoid. If lim+1=0? n>=4. Use if b>n-2 skip return zeros. Fine.

`rebalance_d`:
```
def rebalance_d(p,n,up):
   if n <=1: return zeros
   if up:
      d = np.full(n, -1.0/(n-1)); d[p]=1.0
   else:
      d = np.full(n, 1.0/(n-1)); d[p]=-1.0
   return d
```
Sum exact? up: sum = -(n-1)/(n-1)+1=0. Floating exact? 1/(n-1) times n-1 may not exactly 1 due float, sum small. Good.
Max d 1. For up, if any other near 0, interval hi maybe 0. try_line skip.

`local_search` code. Need `time` imported.
```
def local_search(h,cvec,rng,deadline,max_noimp=2000):
    n=len(h)
    noimp=0; step=0; acc=0
    Kvals=(1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128)
    last = deadline
    while time.time() < deadline:
       step +=1
       # occasional time checks in exp loops? no
       if step % 12 == 0:
           # smooth direction with tau cycle
           tau = (0.5,2.0,10.0,40.0)[(step//12)%4]
           d = smooth_direction(h,cvec,tau)
       elif step % 6 == 0:
           p = (step * 2654435761) % max(1,n-1)
           d = adjacent_d(n,p)
       elif step % 6 == 1:
           b = 2 + ((step//6) % 3)*2  # 2,4,6
           d = block_direction(rng,n,b)
       elif step % 6 == 3:
           if rng.rand() < 0.5:
               p = int(rng.randint(n)); up = h[p] > 0.5
               d = rebalance_d(p,n,up)
           else:
               d = random_pair_d(rng,n)
       else:
           K = Kvals[(step//6) % len(Kvals)]
           g = active_direction(h,cvec,K)
           if step % 2 == 0:
               k = 2 + (step//6) % 8
               d = sparsify_direction(g,k)
           else:
               d = g
       dmax = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
       if dmax < 1e-14:
           d = random_pair_d(rng,n)
       h,cvec,imp = try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline)
       if imp:
          noimp=0; acc+=1
          if acc % 200 == 0:
             h = project_feasible(h,n); cvec=compute_cvec(h,n)
       else:
          noimp +=1
          if noimp >= max_noimp: break
       if time.time() > deadline: break
    return h,cvec
```
Note: In `step % 6 == 1`, we use block; maybe too frequent? Categories: step modulo 12: smooth at 0, adj at 6; mod6 1 block, 3 rebalance/random, 2,4,5? Let's see:
step mod12:
0 smooth
1 block
2 else active
3 reb/rand
4 else active
5 else active
6 adj
7 block
8 active
9 reb/rand
10 active
11 active
So active 4/12, smooth 1/12, adjacent 1/12, block 2/12, reb/rand 2/12. Good. But step odd condition for sparse: active steps 2,4,5,8,10,11; step%2==0 for 2,4,10 -> sparse; 5,11 full. K cycles (step//6)%len: step2->0,4->0,5->0,8->1,10->1,11->1. Good.
- Smooth direction loops all lags; only 1/12. Fine.
- If active_direction returns zero, d random. Good.

`project_direction`:
```
def project_direction(g,h):
   n=len(h)
   g = np.asarray(g, dtype=float)
   g = g - g.mean()
   free = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
   d = -g
   d[~free] = 0.0
   nf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
   if nf == 0: return d
   sf = float(d[free].sum())
   if sf != 0.0:
       d[free] -= sf / nf
   dm = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
   if dm < 1e-14: return d
   return d/dm
```
Note `d[free] -= sf/nf` with boolean works. If sf NaN? no.

Pseudo run:
```
def run(...):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-8; rng=...
 # init h
 init_reserve = int(min(500, max(120, 0.45*budget_s)))
 init_dl = deadline - init_reserve
 if init_dl < t0+20: init_dl = deadline-5
 h_best,cvec_best = local_search(h,cvec,rng,init_dl,5000)
 finalize and update best_h
 # multi n
 n_list = [800,900,1000,600]
 if len(best_h) not in n_list: maybe append len? no
 for nt in n_list:
   if time.time() > deadline - 80: break
   if nt == len(best_h): continue
   remaining = deadline - 80 - time.time()
   if remaining < 40: break
   slice_dl = time.time() + min(90.0, remaining)
   hn=project_feasible(resample_h(best_h,nt),nt)
   cn=compute_cvec(hn,nt)
   hn,cn=local_search(hn,cn,rng,slice_dl,1000)
   finalize; if改进 update best_h
 # noise
 for sigma in (0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05):
   if time.time() > deadline - 60: break
   remaining = deadline - 50 - time.time()
   if remaining < 20: break
   slice_dl = time.time() + min(50.0, remaining)
   nn=len(best_h)
   hn=project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0,sigma,nn),nn)
   cn=compute_cvec(hn,nn)
   hn,cn=local_search(hn,cn,rng,slice_dl,500)
   finalize; update
 # final polish
 if time.time() < deadline - 5:
   place to end
   h=best_h.copy(); c=compute_cvec(...)
   h,c=local_search(h,c,rng,deadline-3,1000)
   finalize; update
 # final project recompute
 h_final=project_feasible(best_h,len(best_h)); cvec=compute_cvec(...); c=...
 return h_final,c,len(h_final)
```

`sparsify_direction`: If `len(idxp) > k`, argsort. If k=0? local passes k>=2. But if k<=0, return zero.
```
def sparsify_direction(d,k):
    if k <= 1: return np.zeros_like(d)
    pos = d > 1e-14; neg = d < -1e-14
    if not np.any(pos) or not np.any(neg): return np.zeros_like(d)
    idxp = np.where(pos)[0]
    if idxp.size > k:
       idxp = idxp[np.argsort(d[idxp], kind='mergesort')[-k:]]
    idxn = np.where(neg)[0]
    if idxn.size > k:
       idxn = idxn[np.argsort(d[idxn], kind='mergesort')[:k]]
    out = np.zeros_like(d)
    out[idxp] = d[idxp]; out[idxn] = d[idxn]
    ps = float(out[idxp].sum()); ns = -float(out[idxn].sum())
    if ps <= 1e-14 or ns <= 1e-14: return out
    out[idxp] *= 1.0/ps; out[idxn] *= -1.0/ns
    dm = max(float(out.max()), -float(out.min()))
    if dm < 1e-14: return out
    return out/dm
```
`kind='mergesort'` optional; to simplify `kind='stable'` may not accept? Use argsort default.

`random_pair_d`: If n==1? Not likely. If n=1, constraints? ignore. Ensure if n<=1 return zeros.

```
p = int(rng.randint(lim+1)); q = int(rng.randint(lim+1))
if q == p:
   q = (p + 1) % (lim+1)
   # if q block starts same? If p+1 <=lim, not same start; okay.
d = np.zeros(n); d[p:p+b]=1; d[q:q+b]=-1
return d
```

```
def lag_gradient(h, idx, n, sign):
    g=np.zeros(n); n1=n-1; s=sign*(idx-n1)
    if s>=0: r=s; nv=n-r; g[:nv]+=1-h[r:]; g[r:]-=h[:nv]
    else: r=-s; nv=n-r; g[r:]+=1-h[:nv]; g[:nv]-=h[r:]
    return g
```

   n=6; h=np.linspace(0.1,0.9,n); eps=1e-6
   # true fd for lag idx=0 maybe? Need idx nonzero.

   errors plus/minus

         err += abs(g[j]-fd)
      if err < best: orient=sign

Calibration code top-level:
```
LAG_ORIENT = 0

def lag_gradient_vec(h, idx, n, sgn):
    g=np.zeros(n); n1=n-1; s=sgn*(idx-n1)
    if s >= 0:
        r=s; nv=n-r
        if nv>0:
            g[:nv] += 1.0 - h[r:]
            g[r:] -= h[:nv]
    else:
        r=-s; nv=n-r
        if nv>0:
            g[r:] += 1.0 - h[:nv]
            g[:nv] -= h[r:]
    return g

def calibrate_lag_orient():
    n=6
    h=np.linspace(0.1,0.9,n)
    c0=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
    idx=0
    # if derivative zero for idx0, try idx=n
    eps=1e-5
    best=1e100; best_sgn=1
    for sgn in (1,-1):
       g=lag_gradient_vec(h,idx,n,sgn)
       err=0.0
       for j in range(n):
           hp=h.copy(); hp[j]+=eps
           cp=np.correlate(hp,1.0-hp,mode='full')
           fd=(cp[idx]-c0[idx])/eps
           err += abs(g[j]-fd)
       if err < best:
           best=err; best_sgn=sgn
    if best > 1.0: # try idx=n endpoint
       idx=n
       c0=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
       best=1e100; best_sgn=1
       for sgn in (1,-1): ...
    return int(best_sgn)

def get_lag_orient():
    global LAG_ORIENT
    if LAG_ORIENT == 0:
        LAG_ORIENT = calibrate_lag_orient()
    return LAG_ORIENT
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
        if diff>0:
            room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*room,0.0,1.0)
        else:
            tot=h.sum()
            if tot<1e-15: break
            h=np.clip(h+(diff/tot)*h,0.0,1.0)
    return h
```
Good.

`compute_cvec`:
```
def compute_cvec(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
```
Line.

`row_max_values`:
```
def row_max_values(ts,A,B,C):
    t=ts[:,None]
    return np.max(C[None,:]+B[None,:]*t+A[None,:]*t*t, axis=1)
```

`line_max_at`:
```
def line_max_at(t,A,B,C):
    return float(np.max(C+B*t+A*t*t))
```

`try_line` code (long). Need ensure `improve_tol` default.

```
def try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline,improve_tol=1e-10):
    n=len(h)
    if time.time()>deadline: return h,cvec,False
    dmax=max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
    if dmax<1e-14: return h,cvec,False
    d=d/dmax
    if abs(float(d.sum()))>1e-7: return h,cvec,False
    pos=d>1e-14; neg=d<-1e-14
    if not np.any(pos) or not np.any(neg): return h,cvec,False
    lo=max(float(np.max(-h[pos]/d[pos])), float(np.max((h[neg]-1.0)/d[neg])))
    hi=min(float(np.min((1.0-h[pos])/d[pos])), float(np.min(h[neg]/(-d[neg]))))
    lo=min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0)
    span=hi-lo
    if not (np.isfinite(lo) and np.isfinite(hi)) or span<1e-8: return h,cvec,False
    t1=lo+0.25*span; t2=lo+0.75*span
    if abs(t1)<1e-12 or abs(t2)<1e-12:
        t1=lo+0.2*span; t2=lo+0.8*span
    if abs(t1)<1e-12 or abs(t2)<1e-12 or abs(t1-t2)<1e-14:
        return h,cvec,False
    h1=h+t1*d; h2=h+t2*d
    if h1.min()< -1e-9 or h1.max()>1.0+1e-9 or h2.min()< -1e-9 or h2.max()>1.0+1e-9:
        return h,cvec,False
    h1=np.clip(h1,0.0,1.0); h2=np.clip(h2,0.0,1.0)
    c1=np.correlate(h1,1.0-h1,mode='full')
    c2=np.correlate(h2,1.0-h2,mode='full')
    C=cvec
    d1=c1-C; d2=c2-C
    A=(d1/t1 - d2/t2)/(t1-t2)
    B=(d1 - A*t1*t1)/t1
    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(A)) and np.all(np.isfinite(B))):
        return h,cvec,False
    n_samp=80 if n<=900 else 60
    ts=np.linspace(lo,hi,n_samp)
    if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
        ts=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts, np.array([0.0]))))
    F0=float(C.max())
    vals=row_max_values(ts,A,B,C)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)): return h,cvec,False
    i0=int(np.argmin(vals))
    m=len(ts)
    if i0==0: lo2,hi2=ts[0],ts[1]
    elif i0==m-1: lo2,hi2=ts[m-2],ts[m-1]
    else: lo2,hi2=ts[i0-1],ts[i0+1]
    if hi2-lo2 <= 1e-12:
        t_best=float(ts[i0])
    else:
        ts2=np.linspace(lo2,hi2,33)
        if lo2 <=0.0<=hi2:
            ts2=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts2, np.array([0.0]))))
        vals2=row_max_values(ts2,A,B,C)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals2)):
            t_best=float(ts[i0])
        else:
            i2=int(np.argmin(vals2))
            m2=len(ts2)
            if i2==0: lo3,hi3=ts2[0],ts2[1]
            elif i2==m2-1: lo3,hi3=ts2[m2-2],ts2[m2-1]
            else: lo3,hi3=ts2[i2-1],ts2[i2+1]
            if hi3-lo3 <= 1e-14:
                t_best=float(ts2[i2])
            else:
                # golden
                phi=(1.0+np.sqrt(5.0))/2.0
                a1=hi3-(hi3-lo3)/phi; a2=lo3+(hi3-lo3)/phi
                f1=line_max_at(a1,A,B,C); f2=line_max_at(a2,A,B,C)
                for _ in range(40):
                    if hi3-lo3 < 1e-8*max(1.0,abs(lo3),abs(hi3)): break
                    if f1 < f2:
                        hi3=a2; a2=a1; f2=f1; a1=hi3-(hi3-lo3)/phi; f1=line_max_at(a1,A,B,C)
                    else:
                        lo3=a1; a1=a2; f1=f2; a2=lo3+(hi3-lo3)/phi; f2=line_max_at(a2,A,B,C)
                t_best=0.5*(lo3+hi3)
    t_best=float(np.clip(t_best,lo,hi))
    f_best=line_max_at(t_best,A,B,C)
    if f_best >= F0 - improve_tol:
        return h,cvec,False
    h_new=h+t_best*d
    if h_new.min()< -1e-9 or h_new.max()>1.0+1e-9:
        return h,cvec,False
    clipped=False
    if h_new.min()<0.0 or h_new.max()>1.0:
        h_new=np.clip(h_new,0.0,1.0); clipped=True
    if clipped or abs(float(h_new.sum())-0.5*n)>1e-8:
        h_new=project_feasible(h_new,n)
        cvec_new=compute_cvec(h_new,n)
    else:
        cvec_new=C+B*t_best+A*t_best*t_best
        cvec_new=np.maximum(cvec_new,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(cvec_new)):
        return h,cvec,False
    new_f=float(cvec_new.max())
    if new_f < F0 - improve_tol:
        return h_new,cvec_new,True
    return h,cvec,False
```
Long but okay. Note if n=1, ts[1] etc? n should >1. If span and len ts at least 2. n_samp>=60.

Now `project_direction`:
```
def project_direction(g,h):
    n=len(h)
    g=g-g.mean()
    free=(h>1e-8)&(h<1.0-1e-8)
    d=-g
    d[~free]=0.0
    nf=int(np.count_nonzero(free))
    if nf==0: return d
    sf=float(d[free].sum())
    if sf != 0.0:
        d[free] -= sf/nf
    dm=max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
    if dm<1e-14: return d
    return d/dm
```
If `g` has nan? active loops no. If `sf` nan? no.

`active_direction`:
```
def active_direction(h,cvec,K):
    n=len(h); m=cvec.size; n1=n-1
    if K <= 0: return np.zeros(n)
    if K >= m:
        idx=np.arange(m); w=1.0/m
    elif K <= 8:
        idx=np.argsort(cvec)[-K:]; w=1.0/K
    else:
        idx=np.argpartition(cvec, m-K)[-K:]; w=1.0/K
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for ii in idx:
        ii=int(ii); wi=w
        if wi <= 0.0: continue
        s=n1-ii
        if s>=0:
            r=s; nv=n-r
            if nv<=0: continue
            g[:nv] += wi*(1.0-h[r:])
            g[r:] -= wi*h[:nv]
        else:
            r=-s; nv=n-r
            if nv<=0: continue
            g[r:] += wi*(1.0-h[:nv])
            g[:nv] -= wi*h[r:]
    return project_direction(g,h)
```
This loops over `idx` from argpartition unsorted; `w` scalar. Good. For K=m, w=1/m. For K small, w=1/K.

`local_search`. Use Kvals maybe `(1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256)`.
```
def local_search(h,cvec,rng,deadline,max_noimp=2000):
    n=len(h)
    if n<2: return h,cvec
    noimp=0; step=0; acc=0
    Kvals=(1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256)
    while time.time()<deadline:
       step+=1
       gmod=(step//6)%len(Kvals)
       if step % 6 == 0:
           p=(step*2654435761) % max(1,n-1); d=adjacent_d(n,p)
       elif step % 6 == 1:
           b=2+((step//6)%3)*2; d=block_direction(rng,n,b)
       elif step % 6 == 3:
           if rng.rand()<0.5:
               p=int(rng.randint(n)); up=bool(h[p]>0.5); d=rebalance_d(p,n,up)
           else:
               d=random_pair_d(rng,n)
       else:
           g=active_direction(h,cvec,Kvals[gmod])
           if step % 2 == 0:
              d=sparsify_direction(g, 2+gmod)
           else:
              d=g
       dm=max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
       if dm < 1e-14:
           d=random_pair_d(rng,n)
       h,cvec,imp=try_line(h,cvec,d,deadline)
       if imp:
           noimp=0; acc+=1
           if acc % 500 == 0:
              h=project_feasible(h,n); cvec=compute_cvec(h,n)
       else:
           noimp+=1
           if noimp>=max_noimp: break
    return h,cvec
```
This uses active for steps mod6 2,4,5 (3/6), block 1, random/reb 3, adjacent 0. Good. Kvals index gmod same for all active group. Sparse k=2+gmod up to 10.

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
        h=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy(); n=len(h)
    except Exception:
        n=600; i=np.arange(n); h=((i%4)<2).astype(float)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cvec=compute_cvec(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy()
    best_c=float(cvec.max()*(2.0/n))
    init_reserve=int(min(500.0, max(120.0, 0.45*budget_s)))
    init_dl=deadline-init_reserve

    if c < best_c:

        now=time.time()

    return h_final, best_c, len(h_final)

```
def block_direction(rng,n,b):
    b=int(b)
    if n < 4 or b < 2: return np.zeros(n)
    if b > n//2: b = max(2, n//2)
    lim=n-b
    p=int(rng.randint(lim+1)); q=int(rng.randint(lim+1))
    if q == p:
        q = (p + 1) % (lim + 1)
    d=np.zeros(n); d[p:p+b]=1.0; d[q:q+b]=-1.0
    return d
```
If p +1 % (lim+1) results same if lim+1=1; but b<=n//2 and n>=4, lim>=b>=2? n//2>=2, lim=n-b>=n-n//2>=2, lim+1>=3. good.

def adjacent_d(n,p):

Try_line compact code:
```
def try_line(...):
  n=len(h)
  if time.time()>deadline: return ...
  dmax=max(float(d.max()),-float(d.min()))
  if dmax<1e-14 or abs(float(d.sum()))>1e-7: return
  d=d/dmax
  pos=d>1e-14; neg=d<-1e-14
  if not (pos.any() and neg.any()): return
  lo=... hi=...
  lo=min(lo,0.0); hi=max(hi,0.0); span=hi-lo
  if not finite or span<1e-8: return
  t1=lo+0.25*span; t2=lo+0.75*span
  if abs(t1)<1e-12 or abs(t2)<1e-12: t1,t2=lo+0.2*span,lo+0.8*span
  if abs(t1)<1e-12 or abs(t1-t2)<1e-14: return
  h1=h+t1*d; h2=h+t2*d
  if not ((h1.min()>-1e-9 and h1.max()<1+1e-9) and ...): return
  c1=correlate(np.clip(h1,0,1),...) etc? We can create h1c=np.clip.
  C=cvec; d1=c1-C; d2=c2-C
  A=... B=...
  if not finite return
  n_samp=101 if n<=900 else 81
  ts=linspace(lo,hi,n_samp); if lo<=0<=hi: ts=unique(concat(ts,[0.0]))
  F0=C.max(); vals=row_max_values(ts,A,B,C)
  if not finite return
  i0=argmin(vals)
  if i0==0: lo3=ts[0]; hi3=ts[1]
  elif i0==len-1: ... else ts[i0-1],ts[i0+1]
  if hi3-lo3>1e-14: golden
     t_best=0.5(...)
  else t_best=ts[i0]
  t_best=clip; f_best=line_max_at
  if not finite or f_best>=F0-tol return
  h_new=h+t_best*d ...
```
This is ~80 lines.

Other trims: project_feasible one-line for final adjust.

Compact local_search/run maybe 50 each. Overall under 300.

Let's write final code with concise style. Ensure no nested functions.

Potential issue in try_line: `pos.any() and neg.any()` in Python with numpy bool works? `and` expects bool; `pos.any()` returns np.bool_, okay. Good.

`c1=np.correlate(np.clip(h1,0.0,1.0), 1.0-np.clip(h1,0.0,1.0), mode='full')` repeats clip. Better:
`h1=np.clip(h1,0.0,1.0)` after range check; same for h2.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = float(h.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if diff > 1e-12:
        j = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
        h[j] = min(1.0, h[j] + diff)
    elif diff < -1e-12:
        j = int(np.argmax(h))
        h[j] = max(0.0, h[j] + diff)
    return h


def compute_cvec(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def row_max_values(ts, A, B, C):
    t = ts[:, None]
    return np.max(C[None, :] + B[None, :] * t + A[None, :] * t * t, axis=1)


def line_max_at(t, A, B, C):
    return float(np.max(C + B * t + A * t * t))


def try_line(h, cvec, d, deadline, improve_tol=1e-10):
    n = len(h)
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return h, cvec, False
    dmax = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
    if dmax < 1e-14 or abs(float(d.sum())) > 1e-7:
        return h, cvec, False
    d = d / dmax
    pos = d > 1e-14
    neg = d < -1e-14
    if not (np.any(pos) and np.any(neg)):
        return h, cvec, False

    lo = max(float(np.max(-h[pos] / d[pos])), float(np.max((h[neg] - 1.0) / d[neg])))
    hi = min(float(np.min((1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos])), float(np.min(h[neg] / (-d[neg]))))
    lo = min(lo, 0.0)
    hi = max(hi, 0.0)
    span = hi - lo
    if not (np.isfinite(lo) and np.isfinite(hi)) or span < 1e-8:
        return h, cvec, False

    t1 = lo + 0.25 * span
    t2 = lo + 0.75 * span
    if abs(t1) < 1e-12 or abs(t2) < 1e-12:
        t1 = lo + 0.2 * span
        t2 = lo + 0.8 * span
    if abs(t1) < 1e-12 or abs(t1 - t2) < 1e-14:
        return h, cvec, False

    h1 = h + t1 * d
    h2 = h + t2 * d
    if (h1.min() < -1e-9 or h1.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9 or
            h2.min() < -1e-9 or h2.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9):
        return h, cvec, False
    h1 = np.clip(h1, 0.0, 1.0)
    h2 = np.clip(h2, 0.0, 1.0)
    c1 = np.correlate(h1, 1.0 - h1, mode="full")
    c2 = np.correlate(h2, 1.0 - h2, mode="full")

    C = cvec
    d1 = c1 - C
    d2 = c2 - C
    A = (d1 / t1 - d2 / t2) / (t1 - t2)
    B = (d1 - A * t1 * t1) / t1
    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(A)) and np.all(np.isfinite(B))):
        return h, cvec, False

    n_samp = 101 if n <= 900 else 81
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, n_samp)
    if lo <= 0.0 <= hi:
        ts = np.unique(np.concatenate((ts, np.array([0.0]))))
    F0 = float(C.max())
    vals = row_max_values(ts, A, B, C)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
        return h, cvec, False

    i0 = int(np.argmin(vals))
    m = len(ts)
    if i0 == 0:
        lo3, hi3 = ts[0], ts[1]
    elif i0 == m - 1:
        lo3, hi3 = ts[m - 2], ts[m - 1]
    else:
        lo3, hi3 = ts[i0 - 1], ts[i0 + 1]

    if hi3 - lo3 > 1e-14:
        phi = (1.0 + np.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
        a1 = hi3 - (hi3 - lo3) / phi
        a2 = lo3 + (hi3 - lo3) / phi
        f1 = line_max_at(a1, A, B, C)
        f2 = line_max_at(a2, A, B, C)
        for _ in range(40):
            if hi3 - lo3 < 1e-8 * max(1.0, abs(lo3), abs(hi3)):
                break
            if f1 < f2:
                hi3 = a2
                a2 = a1
                f2 = f1
                a1 = hi3 - (hi3 - lo3) / phi
                f1 = line_max_at(a1, A, B, C)
            else:
                lo3 = a1
                a1 = a2
                f1 = f2
                a2 = lo3 + (hi3 - lo3) / phi
                f2 = line_max_at(a2, A, B, C)
        t_best = 0.5 * (lo3 + hi3)
        best_f = line_max_at(t_best, A, B, C)
    else:
        t_best = float(ts[i0])
        best_f = line_max_at(t_best, A, B, C)

    for tc in (float(ts[i0]), float(ts[0]), float(ts[-1])):
        if lo <= tc <= hi:
            ff = line_max_at(tc, A, B, C)
            if np.isfinite(ff) and ff < best_f:
                best_f, t_best = ff, tc

    if not np.isfinite(best_f) or best_f >= F0 - improve_tol:
        return h, cvec, False

    h_new = h + t_best * d
    if h_new.min() < -1e-9 or h_new.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9:
        return h, cvec, False
    clipped = False
    if h_new.min() < 0.0 or h_new.max() > 1.0:
        h_new = np.clip(h_new, 0.0, 1.0)
        clipped = True

    if clipped or abs(float(h_new.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-8:
        h_new = project_feasible(h_new, n)
        cvec_new = compute_cvec(h_new, n)
    else:
        cvec_new = C + B * t_best + A * t_best * t_best
        cvec_new = np.maximum(cvec_new, 0.0)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(cvec_new)):
        return h, cvec, False
    new_f = float(cvec_new.max())
    if new_f < F0 - improve_tol:
        return h_new, cvec_new, True
    return h, cvec, False


def project_direction(g, h):
    n = len(h)
    g = g - float(g.mean())
    free = (h > 1e-8) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-8)
    d = -g
    d[~free] = 0.0
    nf = int(np.count_nonzero(free))
    if nf == 0:
        return d
    sf = float(d[free].sum())
    if sf != 0.0:
        d[free] -= sf / nf
    dm = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
    if dm < 1e-14:
        return d
    return d / dm


def active_direction(h, cvec, K):
    n = len(h)
    m = cvec.size
    n1 = n - 1
    if K <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    if K >= m:
        idx = np.arange(m)
        w = 1.0 / m
    elif K <= 8:
        idx = np.argsort(cvec)[-K:]
        w = 1.0 / K
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(cvec, m - K)[-K:]
        w = 1.0 / K

    g = np.zeros(n)
    for ii in idx:
        ii = int(ii)
        s = n1 - ii
        if s >= 0:
            r = s
            nv = n - r
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            g[:nv] += w * (1.0 - h[r:])
            g[r:] -= w * h[:nv]
        else:
            r = -s
            nv = n - r
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            g[r:] += w * (1.0 - h[:nv])
            g[:nv] -= w * h[r:]
    return project_direction(g, h)


def sparsify_direction(d, k):
    if k <= 1:
        return np.zeros_like(d)
    pos = d > 1e-14
    neg = d < -1e-14
    if not (np.any(pos) and np.any(neg)):
        return np.zeros_like(d)

    idxp = np.where(pos)[0]
    if idxp.size > k:
        idxp = idxp[np.argsort(d[idxp])[-k:]]
    idxn = np.where(neg)[0]
    if idxn.size > k:
        idxn = idxn[np.argsort(d[idxn])[:k]]

    out = np.zeros_like(d)
    out[idxp] = d[idxp]
    out[idxn] = d[idxn]
    ps = float(out[idxp].sum())
    ns = -float(out[idxn].sum())
    if ps <= 1e-14 or ns <= 1e-14:
        return out
    out[idxp] *= 1.0 / ps
    out[idxn] *= -1.0 / ns
    dm = max(float(out.max()), -float(out.min()))
    if dm < 1e-14:
        return out
    return out / dm


def random_pair_d(rng, n):
    p = int(rng.randint(n))
    q = int(rng.randint(n))
    if p == q:
        q = (p + 1) % n
    d = np.zeros(n)
    d[p] = 1.0
    d[q] = -1.0
    return d


def adjacent_d(n, p):
    if p >= n - 1:
        p = 0
    d = np.zeros(n)
    d[p] = 1.0
    d[p + 1] = -1.0
    return d


def block_direction(rng, n, b):
    b = int(b)
    if n < 4 or b < 2:
        return np.zeros(n)
    if b > n // 2:
        b = max(2, n // 2)
    lim = n - b
    p = int(rng.randint(lim + 1))
    q = int(rng.randint(lim + 1))
    if q == p:
        q = (p + 1) % (lim + 1)
    d = np.zeros(n)
    d[p:p + b] = 1.0
    d[q:q + b] = -1.0
    return d


def rebalance_d(p, n, up):
    if n < 2:
        return np.zeros(n)
    if up:
        d = np.full(n, -1.0 / (n - 1))
        d[p] = 1.0
    else:
        d = np.full(n, 1.0 / (n - 1))
        d[p] = -1.0
    return d


def resample_h(old, n_new):
    n_old = len(old)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return old.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n_old) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_old)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)


def local_search(h, cvec, rng, deadline, max_noimp=2000):
    n = len(h)
    if n < 2:
        return h, cvec
    noimp = 0
    step = 0
    acc = 0
    Kvals = (1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256)

    while time.time() < deadline:
        step += 1
        gmod = (step // 6) % len(Kvals)

        if step % 6 == 0:
            p = (step * 2654435761) % max(1, n - 1)
            d = adjacent_d(n, p)
        elif step % 6 == 1:
            b = 2 + ((step // 6) % 3) * 2
            d = block_direction(rng, n, b)
        elif step % 6 == 3:
            if rng.rand() < 0.5:
                p = int(rng.randint(n))
                up = bool(h[p] > 0.5)
                d = rebalance_d(p, n, up)
            else:
                d = random_pair_d(rng, n)
        else:
            g = active_direction(h, cvec, Kvals[gmod])
            if step % 2 == 0:
                d = sparsify_direction(g, 2 + gmod)
            else:
                d = g

        dm = max(float(d.max()), -float(d.min()))
        if dm < 1e-14:
            d = random_pair_d(rng, n)

        h, cvec, imp = try_line(h, cvec, d, deadline)
        if imp:
            noimp = 0
            acc += 1
            if acc % 500 == 0:
                h = project_feasible(h, n)
                cvec = compute_cvec(h, n)
        else:
            noimp += 1
            if noimp >= max_noimp:
                break
    return h, cvec


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = len(h)
    except Exception:
        n = 600
        i = np.arange(n)
        h = ((i % 4) < 2).astype(float)

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cvec = compute_cvec(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(cvec.max() * (2.0 / n))

    init_reserve = int(min(500.0, max(120.0, 0.45 * budget_s)))
    init_dl = deadline - init_reserve
    if init_dl <= t0:
        init_dl = deadline
    if init_dl < deadline - 5.0:
        h, cvec = local_search(h, cvec, rng, init_dl, 5000)
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        cvec = compute_cvec(h, n)
        c = float(cvec.max() * (2.0 / n))
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = h.copy(), c, n

    for nt in (800, 900, 1000, 600):
        now = time.time()
        if now > deadline - 90.0 or nt == len(best_h):
            continue
        remaining = deadline - 90.0 - now
        if remaining < 35.0:
            break
        sl = now + min(90.0, remaining)
        hn = project_feasible(resample_h(best_h, nt), nt)
        cn = compute_cvec(hn, nt)
        hn, cn = local_search(hn, cn, rng, sl, 1000)
        hn = project_feasible(hn, nt)
        cn = compute_cvec(hn, nt)
        c = float(cn.max() * (2.0 / nt))
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = hn.copy(), c, nt

    for sigma in (0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05):
        now = time.time()
        if now > deadline - 70.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - 50.0 - now
        if remaining < 20.0:
            break
        sl = now + min(50.0, remaining)
        nl = len(best_h)
        hn = project_feasible(best_h + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, nl), nl)
        cn = compute_cvec(hn, nl)
        hn, cn = local_search(hn, cn, rng, sl, 500)
        hn = project_feasible(hn, nl)
        cn = compute_cvec(hn, nl)
        c = float(cn.max() * (2.0 / nl))
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = hn.copy(), c, nl

    if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
        h = best_h.copy()
        nl = len(h)
        cvec = compute_cvec(h, nl)
        h, cvec = local_search(h, cvec, rng, deadline - 3.0, 1000)
        h = project_feasible(h, nl)
        cvec = compute_cvec(h, nl)
        c = float(cvec.max() * (2.0 / nl))
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, n = h.copy(), c, nl

    h_final = project_feasible(best_h, len(best_h))
    cvec_final = compute_cvec(h_final, len(h_final))
    best_c = float(cvec_final.max() * (2.0 / len(h_final)))
    return h_final, best_c, len(h_final)
```
